
============================================================
COMPUTING METRICS
============================================================

--- RAG-CDB ---
what is the total number of patiemts who had crnry athrscl natve vssl?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT MAX ( DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" ) FROM PROCEDURES WHERE PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "crnry athrscl natve vssl"
{'sel': 'max', 'agg': ['demographic."age"'], 'tab': ['procedures'], 'cond': [['procedures."short_title"', '=', 'crnry athrscl natve vssl']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Crnry athrscl natve vssl"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'crnry athrscl natve vssl']]}
----------

----------

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'single'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'protal hypertension']]}

how many patients follow the protestant quaker religion and were diagnosed with pressure ulcer, stage i?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" = DIAGNOSES."SUBJECT_ID" 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."RELIGION" = "PROTESTANT QUAKER" 
AND DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Pressure ulcer, stage I"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."religion"', '=', 'protestant quaker'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'pressure ulcer, stage i']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."RELIGION" = "PROTESTANT QUAKER" AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Pressure ulcer, stage I"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."religion"', '=', 'protestant quaker'], ['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'pressure ulcer, stage i']]}
----------

PREDS:

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."dob_year"', '<', '2107'], ['demographic."ethnicity"', '=', 'hispanic/latino - puertorican']]}

TRUTH:

provide the number of dead patients who were less than 43 years of age.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DISCHARGE_LOCATION" = "DEAD/EXPIRED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < "43"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."age"', '<', '43'], ['demographic."discharge_location"', '=', 'dead/expired']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "1" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < "43"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."age"', '<', '43'], ['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '1']]}
----------

How many patients have gastrointestinal bleed and tp drug route?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Gastrointestinal bleed" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."ROUTE" = "TP"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'gastrointestinal bleed'], ['prescriptions."route"', '=', 'tp']]}
----------
TRUTH:

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'gastrointestinal bleed'], ['prescriptions."route"', '=', 'tp']]}

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."days_stay"', '>', '23'], ['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'phenylephrine hcl']]}

----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "FEMORAL ARTERY THROMBOSIS" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DOD_YEAR" <= "2154"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'femoral artery thrombosis'], ['demographic."dod_year"', '<=', '2154']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "FEMORAL ARTERY THROMBOSIS" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DOD_YEAR" <= "2154.0"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'femoral artery thrombosis'], ['demographic."dod_year"', '<=', '2154.0']]}

how many of the patients with sepsis as primary disease had icd9 code 9960?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" = "9960" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = 'sepsis'
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'sepsis'], ['diagnoses."icd9_code"', '=', '9960']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "SEPSIS" AND PROCEDURES."ICD9_CODE" = "9960"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'sepsis'], ['procedures."icd9_code"', '=', '9960']]}

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admityear"', '<', '2129'], ['lab."label"', '=', 'rbc, pleural']]}
